<details open><summary>🔧 Tool call — mcp__agent-ml-research__reviewer_post_to_director · `toolu_01Xe3JE1HsGrug6qGRusyXe4`</summary>

````json
{
  "text": "[severity: warning] BPE 碰撞组发现的论文呈现策略 — 审查意见\n\n## 核心问题：分析方法论缺陷\n\n`analyze_ac_violations.py` 使用**硬编码**的 Mistral SentencePiece 碰撞组（SEN/SID/SIDE, BAL/BOD, ABS/AML, PLA/PTH）来分析所有三个模型。但实际代码 `type_mask_processor.py:412` 的 `_build_entity_type_collision_groups()` 是**动态检测**的——基于每个 tokenizer 的 first-token 映射：\n\n```python\ndef _build_entity_type_collision_groups(self):\n    groups = defaultdict(list)\n    for etype, tid in self._type_first_token.items():\n        groups[tid].append(etype)\n    return {tid: etypes for tid, etypes in groups.items() if len(etypes) > 1}\n```\n\n**结论**：Agent 报告的 \"Qwen SEN/SID/SIDE 碰撞 34 次\" 和 \"Llama BOD/BAL 碰撞 76 次\" 使用的是 Mistral 的碰撞组定义，不能证明 BPE tokenizer 存在相同的首 token 碰撞。\n\n## 在回答三个问题之前，必须先做一个诊断\n\n**必做验证**（5 分钟，不需要 GPU）：对 Qwen/Llama tokenizer 运行 `_build_entity_type_collision_groups()` 或等效的 one-liner，输出 BPE 下的实际碰撞组。两种可能结果：\n\n**情况 A — BPE 确实存在首 token 碰撞**（相同或不同的组）：\n- 说明碰撞是跨 tokenizer 架构的通用问题，continuation mask 可能可以扩展覆盖\n- v4 rerun 有意义，但优先级低于 70B（见下）\n- 论文呈现选 (B)：在 error analysis 中展示碰撞组的跨 tokenizer 分析，说明 \"Mistral 已修复，BPE 碰撞组不同但机制相同，future work\"\n\n**情况 B — BPE 无首 token 碰撞**（更可能）：\n- Qwen/Llama 的 AC 违规是**模型语义混淆**（SEN vs SID 语义接近），不是 tokenizer 碰撞\n- continuation mask 对此无效，v4 rerun 毫无意义\n- 论文呈现选 (C)：error analysis 中简述 \"residual AC 来自语义相近类型的混淆（非 tokenizer 碰撞），与 SentencePiece 碰撞机制不同\"\n\n## 关于 v4 rerun\n\n**无论诊断结果如何，不建议现在做 v4**：\n- 70B 实验（model scaling 维度）是 EMNLP 论文的核心缺口，优先级远高于 0.8-1.7pp 的 AC 优化\n- 当前三模型 go_no_go 已在 1.26-3.8%，足以支撑 \"near-zero violation\" claim\n- 0.8pp 改善在无统计检验的情况下可能是噪声\n\n## 建议行动\n\n1. **立即**：让 Worker 对 Qwen/Llama tokenizer 运行碰撞组诊断（5min，无 GPU）\n2. **根据诊断结果**选择 (B) 或 (C) 的论文呈现\n3. **不做 v4 rerun**，GPU 时间全部给 70B",
  "project": "schema_semantic_constrained_ie"
}

128	- BAL → BALL / BOD → BODY

Excellent! This is a very thorough and high-quality diagnosis. Let me analyze the key findings:

**Critical finding: BPE tokenizers (Qwen, Llama) have ZERO collision groups.**

- Qwen: 0 bare vs generation-context mismatches, 0 collision groups
- Llama: 0 bare vs generation-context mismatches, 0 collision groups
- Mistral (SentencePiece): 2 collision groups in generation context (SEN/SID/SIDE, BAL/BOD)

This confirms my hypothesis in the earlier report to Director:


The collision counts in `analyze_ac_violations.py` were incorrectly applying Mistral's hardcoded collision groups to Qwen and Llama, so those AC percentages aren't actually caused by tokenizer collisions at all—they stem from grammar-first fallback behavior and position detection edge cases instead. The continuation mask fix only addresses Mistral's SentencePiece tokenizer, making a v4 rerun for BPE models unnecessary since there are no collisions to fix.

For Mistral specifically, the collision mechanism is clear: SEN/SID/SIDE all start with token 'S' and BAL/BOD both start with 'B' in the generation context, with 42% of constraints partially overlapping these collision groups. The continuation mask (D047) has already proven effective, dropping Mistral's AC from 16.6% down to 1.3%, so the remaining violations are likely edge cases the mask doesn't fully address.

Now I need to reconsider how to present this to the Director—the BPE collision diagnosis requires a different approach.

Let me analyze the ROADMAP update:

**Key updates in ROADMAP**:

1. **v3 results integrated**: All three models' v3 numbers now included. BPE diagnostic result also integrated: "Qwen/Llama 0 collision groups, Mistral 2 groups. Residual AC = model semantic confusion" — this reflects my recommendation from the BPE diagnostic review. Good.

2. **Paper Agent**: Round 5 score = "Weak Accept (Findings)". Paper Agent is fixing 17 open issues from review (2 critical, 11 warning, 4 info + new §5.4 AC narrative correction).
   
3. **Conference Main 补强计划 (D049)**: The priority breakdown shows 70B experiments as the critical blocker still waiting for download, while the EE post-hoc baseline and CF majority voting are complete. Error analysis depends on the v3 results that just came through, and there's some optional work on EE FT composability and grammar-only cross-model comparisons if time allows.

4. **GPU scheduling**: All four GPUs are sitting idle waiting for the 70B model to arrive, but once it does I'm planning to run 4-way parallel experiments on SciERC and DocRED across both servers.

5. **Timeline**: The schedule is tight—70B launch and Paper Agent fixes today, internal draft and review tomorrow, then 70B results and error analysis by May 20th, with review iterations through the 23rd before finalizing and submitting on the 25th.

6. **GPU budget and download status**: I've used about 45 GPU-hours so far with 50-80 hours remaining on the 4x RTX PRO 6000 setup. The 70B download is still stuck on shard 009 in temp—the process has been running for over an hour and a half now, which is concerning.

I need to check the actual size of shard 009 to understand why it's taking so long. There's also a data inconsistency in the ROADMAP where D050 shows Mistral AC dropping to 0%, but D051 corrected it to 1.3%—I should verify if that's just a truncation issue or an actual error. On the timeline front, getting the 70B model onto both servers is critical since the experiments need it on westd-21461 and westd-16980, and the SCP transfer across the LAN should only take 10-15 minutes for the 37GB file.

For the 70B Q-INT4 inference, the model should fit on a single 96GB GPU, and running both SciERC and DocRED with 50 docs each will probably take 1-2 hours per experiment, but 4-way parallelization across 4 GPUs is definitely doable. I also need to verify the C7 claim about cross-task generalization since the paper says it's "upgraded to supported/strong" but I should check if the v3 results actually support that for EE across the three models. Finally, Round 5 being marked as "Weak Accept (Findings)" is just the internal Paper Agent evaluation, not actual reviewer feedback, so I need to keep that context in mind for the conference submission.

The 17 fixes plus 70B results should push this toward Main rather than Findings. The BPE diagnostic findings about collision groups in Qwen/Llama versus Mistral's 2 groups, and how residual AC reflects model semantic confusion, all check out with what I've documented. I'm noticing the roadmap is missing a couple of things I flagged earlier—the status on Llama/Mistral unconstrained posthoc violation reports and the naming issue with violation_report_ee.json. On the GPU side, I've got 50-80 hours of compute needed and 4 GPUs available, which translates to 12.5-20 hours of wall time, and with 7 days until the May 25 deadline, there's no risk even accounting for 70B being slower. Now I'm checking on the Shard 009 download status.

### Tool result

_source line 3590 · 2026-05-18T12:10:51.644Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_018N5tz2cawaSWUqrNinFE6u`</summary>

````text
1	# Beam Search / Sampling 可行性检查报告
2	
3	## 结论
4	
5	| 方法 | 可行性 | 说明 |
6	|------|--------|------|
7	| **Sampling** (temperature/top_p/top_k) | **YES** | batch_size=1 下无需改代码，只需加 CLI 参数 |
8	| **Beam Search** (num_beams > 1) | **NEEDS_CODE_CHANGE** | TypeConstraintProcessor 单一共享 state 是 blocker |
9	
10	---
11	
12	## 1. 当前代码现状
13	
14	### 参数支持
15	
16	`run_constrained_extraction.py` (L602-615) argparse 中**无任何** beam/sampling 参数：
17	
18	```python
19	parser.add_argument("--max_new_tokens", type=int, default=2048)
20	# 无 num_beams, do_sample, temperature, top_p, top_k
21	```
22	
23	所有 `generate()` 调用（L441, L451, L483, L494, L542, L553）均硬编码 `do_sample=False`（greedy）。
24	
25	### generate() 调用方式
26	
27	`run_constrained_extraction.py:436-441`:
28	```python
29	gen_kwargs = dict(**inputs, max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens, do_sample=False)
30	if processors:
31	    gen_kwargs["logits_processor"] = processors
32	with torch.no_grad():
33	    output_ids = model.generate(**gen_kwargs)
34	```
35	
36	---
37	
38	## 2. XGrammar Layer — 多 beam 兼容性 ✅
39	
40	`/root/miniconda3/lib/python3.12/site-packages/xgrammar/contrib/hf.py`:
41	
42	```python
43	def __call__(self, input_ids, scores):
44	    if len(self.matchers) == 0:
45	        self.batch_size = input_ids.shape[0]  # 自动适配 beam 数
46	        self.matchers = [
47	            xgr.GrammarMatcher(self.compiled_grammars[i]) for i in range(self.batch_size)
48	        ]
49	```
50	
51	XGrammar 在首次调用时根据 `input_ids.shape[0]`（beam search 下 = num_beams）创建**独立的 GrammarMatcher per beam**。每个 matcher 独立追踪 grammar state。**完全兼容 beam search。**
52	
53	---
54	
55	## 3. TypeConstraintProcessor (Semantic Layer) — Beam Search Blocker ❌
56	
57	`compiler/type_mask_processor.py:188-200`:
58	```python
59	class TypeConstraintProcessor(LogitsProcessor):
60	    def __init__(self, spec, tokenizer, prompt_length=0):
61	        self.state = GenerationState()  # ← 单一 state 实例
62	```
63	
64	`__call__` (L238-272):
65	```python
66	def __call__(self, input_ids, scores):
67	    batch_size = input_ids.shape[0]
68	    for batch_idx in range(batch_size):
69	        generated_text = self.tokenizer.decode(input_ids[batch_idx, self.prompt_length:].tolist(), ...)
70	        position = self._detect_position(generated_text)  # ← 修改 self.state
71	        if position == FieldPosition.RELATION_HEAD_TYPE:
72	            scores[batch_idx] = self._mask_head_type(scores[batch_idx])  # ← 读 self.state
73	```
74	
75	**问题**：`_detect_position()` 调用 `PositionDetector.detect(generated_text, self.state)` 会**修改** `self.state`（entity_registry、current_relation_type、current_head_type 等）。Beam search 下 batch_size = num_beams，循环中每个 beam 的 parsing 都会**覆盖**同一个 state。
76	
77	具体影响：
78	- Beam 0 注册 entity "BERT" → type "Method"，写入 `self.state.entity_registry`
79	- Beam 1 解析时可能尚未生成 "BERT"，但 registry 已污染
80	- `_mask_head_type()` / `_mask_tail_type()` 读取 `self.state.current_relation_type` 时得到的是最后一个 beam 的值
81	
82	---
83	
84	## 4. Sampling 的可行性
85	
86	Sampling（`do_sample=True, temperature=T, top_p=P`）在 **batch_size=1** 下执行，`input_ids.shape[0]` 始终为 1，不存在 state 冲突。
87	
88	**所需改动（纯参数传递，不涉及 processor 逻辑）：**
89	
90	```python
91	# argparse 加 3 个参数
92	parser.add_argument("--do_sample", action="store_true")
93	parser.add_argument("--temperature", type=float, default=1.0)
94	parser.add_argument("--top_p", type=float, default=1.0)
95	
96	# generate() 调用改为
97	gen_kwargs = dict(**inputs, max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,
98	                  do_sample=args.do_sample,
99	                  temperature=args.temperature,
100	                  top_p=args.top_p)
101	```
102	
103	改动量：~10 行。
104	
105	---
106	
107	## 5. Beam Search 所需改动
108	
109	### 核心改动：per-beam state
110	
111	`TypeConstraintProcessor` 需要维护 `List[GenerationState]` 而非单一 `GenerationState`：
112	
113	```python
114	class TypeConstraintProcessor(LogitsProcessor):
115	    def __init__(self, spec, tokenizer, prompt_length=0, num_beams=1):
116	        self.states = [GenerationState() for _ in range(num_beams)]
117	
118	    def __call__(self, input_ids, scores):
119	        if len(self.states) < input_ids.shape[0]:
120	            # 延迟初始化到实际 beam 数
121	            self.states = [GenerationState() for _ in range(input_ids.shape[0])]
122	
123	        for batch_idx in range(input_ids.shape[0]):
124	            generated_text = self.tokenizer.decode(...)
125	            position = self.detector.detect(generated_text, self.states[batch_idx])
126	            # masking 函数也需接受 state 参数
127	            if position == FieldPosition.RELATION_HEAD_TYPE:
128	                scores[batch_idx] = self._mask_head_type(scores[batch_idx], self.states[batch_idx])
129	```
130	
131	### 改动清单
132	
133	| 文件 | 改动 |
134	|------|------|
135	| `compiler/type_mask_processor.py` | `self.state` → `self.states: List[GenerationState]`；`_mask_head_type`/`_mask_tail_type` 加 state 参数 |
136	| `compiler/type_mask_processor_cf_fallback.py` | 同上（相同结构） |
137	| `run_constrained_extraction.py` | 加 `--num_beams` 参数，传入 generate() |
138	
139	### 改动量估算
140	
141	- `type_mask_processor.py`: ~30 行改动（state 初始化 + 函数签名 + 索引）
142	- `type_mask_processor_cf_fallback.py`: ~30 行（相同改动）
143	- `run_constrained_extraction.py`: ~15 行（argparse + gen_kwargs）
144	- **总计：~75 行**
145	
146	### 额外注意
147	
148	- HF `generate()` 在 beam search 中可能做 beam reordering（选出 top-k beams 后重排序）。此时 `input_ids` 的顺序会变化，`states` 列表需要对应重排。不过由于 `PositionDetector.detect()` 每步都从完整 generated_text 重新解析（无增量 state），所以 reordering 不会导致 state 不一致——每步都是从文本重建 state。**这意味着 per-beam state 实际上不需要跨步维护**，因为 detect() 每次 full re-parse。
149	- 但 `entity_registry` 是累积的——每步 regex finditer 全文，所以即使 beam reorder 也能正确重建。
150	
151	**结论：改动后 beam search 直接可行，无需处理 beam reorder。**
152	
153	---
154	
155	## 6. 总结
156	
157	| 场景 | 可行性 | 改动量 | 风险 |
158	|------|--------|--------|------|
159	| Sampling (temp/top_p) | ✅ 立即可用 | ~10 行 | 无 |
160	| Beam search (num_beams) | ⚠️ 需改代码 | ~75 行 | 低（detect() 每步全文 re-parse，无增量 state 依赖） |
161	
162	**推荐回复 reviewer**：sampling 已支持（或极易添加），beam search 需约 75 行改动使 TypeConstraintProcessor 支持 per-beam state，架构上无阻塞。
163	
````

XGrammar Layer — 多 beam 兼容性 ✅\n\n`/root/miniconda3/lib/python3.12/site-packages/xgrammar/contrib/hf.py`:\n\n```python\ndef __call__(self, input_ids, scores):\n    if len(self.matchers) == 0:\n        self.batch_size = input_ids.shape[0]  # 自动适配 beam 数\n        self.matchers = [\n            xgr.GrammarMatcher(self.compiled_grammars[i]) for i in range(self.batch_size)\n        ]\n```\n\nXGrammar 在首次调用时根据 `input_ids.shape[0]`（beam search 下 = num_beams）创建**独立的 GrammarMatcher per beam**。每个 matcher 独立追踪 grammar state。**完全兼容 beam search。**\n\n---\n\n## 3. TypeConstraintProcessor (Semantic Layer) — Beam Search Blocker ❌\n\n`compiler/type_mask_processor.py:188-200`:\n```python\nclass TypeConstraintProcessor(LogitsProcessor):\n    def __init__(self, spec, tokenizer, prompt_length=0):\n        self.state = GenerationState()  # ← 单一 state 实例\n```\n\n`__call__` (L238-272):\n```python\ndef __call__(self, input_ids, scores):\n    batch_size = input_ids.shape[0]\n    for batch_idx in range(batch_size):\n        generated_text = self.tokenizer.decode(input_ids[batch_idx, self.prompt_length:].tolist(), ...)\n        position = self._detect_position(generated_text)  # ← 修改 self.state\n        if position == FieldPosition.RELATION_HEAD_TYPE:\n            scores[batch_idx] = self._mask_head_type(scores[batch_idx])  # ← 读 self.state\n```\n\n**问题**：`_detect_position()` 调用 `PositionDetector.detect(generated_text, self.state)` 会**修改** `self.state`（entity_registry、current_relation_type、current_head_type 等）。Beam search 下 batch_size = num_beams，循环中每个 beam 的 parsing 都会**覆盖**同一个 state。\n\n具体影响：\n- Beam 0 注册 entity \"BERT\" → type \"Method\"，写入 `self.state.entity_registry`\n- Beam 1 解析时可能尚未生成 \"BERT\"，但 registry 已污染\n- `_mask_head_type()` / `_mask_tail_type()` 读取 `self.state.current_relation_type` 时得到的是最后一个 beam 的值\n\n---\n\n## 4. Sampling 的可行性\n\nSampling（`do_sample=True, temperature=T, top_p=P`）在 **batch_size=1** 下执行，`input_ids.shape[0]` 始终为 1，不存在 state 冲突。\n\n**所需改动（纯参数传递，不涉及 processor 逻辑）：**\n\n```python\n# argparse 加 3 个参数\nparser.add_argument(\"--do_sample\", action=\"store_true\")\nparser.add_argument(\"--temperature\", type=float, default=1.0)\nparser.add_argument(\"--top_p\", type=float, default=1.0)\n\n# generate() 调用改为\ngen_kwargs = dict(**inputs, max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,\n                  do_sample=args.do_sample,\n                  temperature=args.temperature,\n                  top_p=args.top_p)\n```\n\n改动量：~10 行。\n\n---\n\n## 5. Beam Search 所需改动\n\n### 核心改动：per-beam state\n\n`TypeConstraintProcessor` 需要维护 `List[GenerationState]` 而非单一 `GenerationState`：\n\n```python\nclass TypeConstraintProcessor(LogitsProcessor):\n    def __init__(self, spec, tokenizer, prompt_length=0, num_beams=1):\n        self.states = [GenerationState() for _ in range(num_beams)]\n\n    def __call__(self, input_ids, scores):\n        if len(self.states) < input_ids.shape[0]:\n            # 延迟初始化到实际 beam 数\n            self.states = [GenerationState() for _ in range(input_ids.shape[0])]\n\n        for batch_idx in range(input_ids.shape[0]):\n            generated_text = self.tokenizer.decode(...)\n            position = self.detector.detect(generated_text, self.states[batch_idx])\n            # masking 函数也需接受 state 参数\n            if position == FieldPosition.RELATION_HEAD_TYPE:\n                scores[batch_idx] = self._mask_head_type(scores[batch_idx], self.states[batch_idx])\n```\n\n### 改动清单\n\n| 文件 | 改动 |\n|------|------|\n| `compiler/type_mask_processor.py` | `self.state` → `self.states: List[GenerationState]`；`_mask_head_type`/`_mask_tail_type` 加 state 参数 |\n| `compiler/type_mask_processor_cf_fallback.py` | 同上（相同结构） |\n| `run_constrained_extraction.py` | 加 `--num_beams` 参数，传入 generate() |\n\n### 改动量估算\n\n- `type_mask_processor.py`: ~30 行改动（state 初始化 + 函数签名 + 索引）\n- `type_mask_processor_cf_fallback.py`: ~30 行（相同改动）\n- `run_constrained_extraction.py`: ~15 行（argparse + gen_kwargs）\n- **总计：~75 行**\n\n### 额外注意\n\n- HF `generate()` 在 beam search 中可能做 beam reordering（选出 top-k beams 后重排序）。此时 `input_ids` 的顺序会变化，`states` 列表需要对应重排。不过由于 `PositionDetector.detect()` 每步都从完整 generated_text 重新解析（无增量 state），所以 reordering 不会导致 state 不一致——每步都是从文本重建 state。**这意味着 per-beam state 实际上不需要跨步维护**，因为 detect() 每次 full re-parse。\n- 但 `entity_registry` 是累积的——每步 regex finditer 全文，所以即使 beam reorder 也能正确重建。\n\n**结论：改动后 beam search 直接可行，无需处理 beam reorder。**\n\n---\n\n## 6. 总结\n\n| 场景 | 可行性 | 改动量 | 风险 |\n|------|--------|--------|------|\n| Sampling (temp/top_p) | ✅ 立即可用 | ~10 行 | 无 |\n| Beam search (num_beams) | ⚠️ 需改代码 | ~75 行 | 低（detect() 每步全文 re-parse，无增量 state 依赖） |\n\n**推荐回复 reviewer**：sampling 已支持（或极易添加），beam search 需约 75 行改动使 TypeConstraintProcessor 支持 per-beam state，架构上无阻塞。\n",

---

**审稿报告摘要：**
- 新 critical 1 项 (MF1: "zero violations" overclaim)
- 携带 warning 3 项 (SF1 beam in appendix, SF2 -ing phrases, SF3 DocRED coverage)
- Info 3 项 (M1 parse failure downgrade, M2 Contrib#3 density, M3 dual-factor caveat)

---

**我的 Action Matrix：**

**MF1 [critical, 必做]**: "zero violations" claim scoping
- (a) 即刻：Abstract line 13 限定为 "on SciERC (Qwen3-8B)" 或改为 "near-zero across configs, with complete elimination on SciERC+Qwen"。§1 Contribution #3 同步。
- (b) 补实验：70B 后跑 Llama SciERC LoRA+beam k=4（~1-2h），若零违规则升级 claim。
- 问题请审：(a) 的改写是否过于保守？"near-zero" vs 具体 scoped claim 哪个更好？

**SF1 [warning]**: §5 新增 1-2 段 beam search 讨论（SciERC beam k=4 DR=0% CF=0.53%）
- 问题请审：放在 §5 末尾还是 §7 Discussion 更合适？

**SF2 [warning]**: -ing 改写 7-8 处
- 方案：逐处改写，Abstract 2处 + §1 2处 + §5 4处 "confirming" 改为不同句式
- 问题请审：改写后是否会引入新的 AI 风格标志？

**SF3 [info→warning]**: DocRED coverage 显式报告
- 方案：基于 fallback rate 10.2% 报告 effective coverage ≈89.8%
- 问题请审：这个数字的计算逻辑是否正确（100% - fallback rate = effective coverage）？

**M1 [info]**: 不再追加动作（共识降级，R2-N3 已有 parse-excluded 分析）

**M2 [info]**: Contribution #3 拆分为两条（与 MF1 合并处理）

**M3 [info]**: §4 加 n=4 caveat 一句

**Q1**: Llama beam+LoRA 排队（70B 后）

**Q2**: DocRED beam 扩大 subset — 低优先级，时间允许才做

---

**战略判断请审：**
1. 路径到 4.0 Conference：MF1(必做) + SF1(beam进正文) + SF2(-ing) + 70B 结果 → 是否足够？
2. 70B 实验完成后，是否应优先跑 Llama beam+LoRA (Q1) 而非 P3 error analysis？Q1 直接影响 Abstract 措辞强度。
3. 时间线：May 25 DDL，MF1+SF1+SF2+SF3+M2+M3 全部是写作任务可并行 Paper Agent，70B 在跑，是否有遗漏的时间风险？

```text
This is a massive amount of data. Let me now analyze the "zero violations" claim systematically.

**MF1: "zero violations" overclaim analysis**

Looking at the registry data, which experiments actually achieve truly zero violations?

**SciERC (RE task):**
- `beam_search_scierc_full_551` (Qwen ZS, beam k=4): ET=0, RV=0, DR=0, CF=0.53% → **NOT zero** (CF violations)
- `w10_beam_ablation_k8` (Qwen ZS, beam k=8): ET=0, RV=0, DR=0, CF=0.08% → **NOT zero** (CF violations)


- `w10_qwen_scierc_ft_beam_4` (Qwen FT, beam k=4): **ALL ZERO** — ET=0, RV=0, DR=0, CF=0 → **TRUE ZERO** ✅
- `w9_llama_scierc_beam_551` (Llama ZS, beam k=4): ET=0, RV=0, DR=0, CF=0.93% → **NOT zero**
- `phase2_scierc_full_s1` (Qwen ZS, greedy): ET=0, RV=0, DR=0.93%, CF=9.9% → Not zero

So far only one experiment truly hits zero across all violation types, while most others have at least some constraint failures even when other metrics look clean. Now I'm looking at the DocRED results with various configurations—Qwen and Llama models using beam search and fine-tuning approaches, where most show minimal error types but consistently have some hallucination or constraint violation issues, with the greedy Qwen variant showing notably higher deletion rates.

For WikiEvents, all three model families (Qwen, Llama, Mistral) achieve zero violations on the first three metrics but have substantial constraint violation rates ranging from 14-21%. Across all these experiments, only one configuration actually achieves true zero violations across every metric: the Qwen fine-tuned model with beam search on SciERC, which makes the reviewer's point about zero violations claims quite valid.

Now looking at how to scope the claims properly, the Director's proposed framing needs refinement. The data shows that "complete elimination" isn't just about using Qwen on SciERC—it specifically requires the fine-tuned version with beam search k=4. Other combinations like Qwen fine-tuned with greedy decoding or Qwen zero-shot with beam search still have constraint violations, so the scope needs to be much more precise than just naming the dataset and model family.

For the "near-zero across configs" claim, I'm examining the actual ranges across different tasks and configurations, and they're quite wide—from 0% up to 33.2% depending on the model, decoding strategy, and dataset. That's not really near-zero in any meaningful sense.

A more accurate framing would be that the approach eliminates ET/RV violations consistently, but the go_no_go metric varies significantly—it's 0% on SciERC with beam k≥4, but climbs to 0.2-2.6% on DocRED and 1-4% on WikiEvents.

For the beam results, §5 末尾 is the right place since these are experimental findings, not limitations or future directions. And I need to clarify what "fallback rate" actually refers to in the metrics—it looks like it's tracking how often the grammar-first approach falls back to another strategy, so the effective coverage would be 100% minus that fallback rate. Looking at the DocRED metrics, the 10.21% DR violation rate maps directly to the 89.8% figure the Director cited, suggesting they're framing violation rate as the inverse of "effective coverage." But this terminology is misleading—the system processes all triples, so it's more accurate to say it achieves DR compliance on 89.8% rather than claiming effective coverage of that percentage. The Director might also be referring to coverage across DocRED's relation types specifically, where some relations may not be fully constrained.

For the path forward, MF1 is the critical bottleneck—the reviewer indicated fixing it could push the score to ~4.0, while SF1 and SF2 are refinements. The 70B results would provide model scaling evidence. This trajectory seems viable if MF1 is properly scoped and the larger model results are positive. Between Q1 (Llama beam+LoRA) and P3 (error analysis), Q1 is the more direct approach.

Q1 directly strengthens the Abstract claim if it achieves zero violations on another model, whereas P3 adds analytical depth without changing the core claims. Timeline-wise, the writing tasks can run in parallel with the 70B experiments, so there's no bottleneck as long as the larger model finishes within roughly 12 hours on the current GPU setup.
```

My role is to independently verify experiment conclusions, check GPU resource compliance (L1 red line #4), audit ROADMAP changes, review Worker outputs, and report findings to Director via `reviewer_post_to_director`. 2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **OntoCoDec**: Two-layer constrained decoding (Grammar via XGrammar + Semantic via TypeConstraintProcessor)
   - **AWQ INT4 quantization**: 4-bit quantized 70B model, ~37GB total, 9 safetensors shards
   - **ModelScope snapshot_download**: Downloads to `._____temp/` directory, moves completed files to main directory one by one. Uses `___1` instead of `.1` in path naming.
   - **Collision groups**: Entity types sharing the same first token in a tokenizer. SentencePiece (Mistral) has 2 groups (SEN/SID/SIDE, BAL/BOD). BPE (Qwen, Llama) has 0 groups — confirmed by diagnostic.
   - **`_build_entity_type_collision_groups()`**: Dynamic per-tokenizer collision detection in `type_mask_processor.py:412`
   - **`analyze_ac_violations.py`**: Uses HARDCODED Mistral collision groups — methodology flaw when applied to BPE models
   - **go_no_go metric**: (RoV + AC) per-argument violation rate for EE; DR-based for RE
   - **Beam search feasibility**: XGrammar supports multi-beam natively; TypeConstraintProcessor needs ~75 lines for per-beam state; Sampling works immediately (~10 lines)
   - **Round 6 score**: 3.5 Borderline Conference. MF1 "zero violations" overclaim is critical fix. Reviewer says fixing MF1 could push to ~4.0.
   - **True zero violations**: Only `w10_qwen_scierc_ft_beam_4` (Qwen + LoRA FT + beam k=4 on SciERC) achieves all-zero across ET/RV/DR/CF

       def _build_entity_type_collision_groups(self) -> dict[int, list[str]]:

               groups[tid].append(etype)

        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_6/projects/schema_semantic_constrained_ie/artifacts/beam_sampling_feasibility.md",
        "content": "# Beam Search / Sampling 可行性检查报告\n\n## 结论\n\n| 方法 | 可行性 | 说明 |\n|------|--------|------|\n| **Sampling** (temperature/top_p/top_k) | **YES** | batch_size=1 下无需改代码，只需加 CLI 参数 |\n| **Beam Search** (num_beams > 1) | **NEEDS_CODE_CHANGE** | TypeConstraintProcessor 单一共享 state 是 blocker |\n\n---\n\n## 1. 当前代码现状\n\n### 参数支持\n\n`run_constrained_extraction.py` (L602-615) argparse 中**无任何** beam/sampling 参数：\n\n```python\nparser.add_argument(\"--max_new_tokens\", type=int, default=2048)\n# 无 num_beams, do_sample, temperature, top_p, top_k\n```\n\n所有 `generate()` 调用（L441, L451, L483, L494, L542, L553）均硬编码 `do_sample=False`（greedy）。\n\n### generate() 调用方式\n\n`run_constrained_extraction.py:436-441`:\n```python\ngen_kwargs = dict(**inputs, max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens, do_sample=False)\nif processors:\n    gen_kwargs[\"logits_processor\"] = processors\nwith torch.no_grad():\n    output_ids = model.generate(**gen_kwargs)\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. XGrammar Layer — 多 beam 兼容性 ✅\n\n`/root/miniconda3/lib/python3.12/site-packages/xgrammar/contrib/hf.py`:\n\n```python\ndef __call__(self, input_ids, scores):\n    if len(self.matchers) == 0:\n        self.batch_size = input_ids.shape[0]  # 自动适配 beam 数\n        self.matchers = [\n            xgr.GrammarMatcher(self.compiled_grammars[i]) for i in range(self.batch_size)\n        ]\n```\n\nXGrammar 在首次调用时根据 `input_ids.shape[0]`（beam search 下 = num_beams）创建**独立的 GrammarMatcher per beam**。每个 matcher 独立追踪 grammar state。**完全兼容 beam search。**\n\n---\n\n## 3. TypeConstraintProcessor (Semantic Layer) — Beam Search Blocker ❌\n\n`compiler/type_mask_processor.py:188-200`:\n```python\nclass TypeConstraintProcessor(LogitsProcessor):\n    def __init__(self, spec, tokenizer, prompt_length=0):\n        self.state = GenerationState()  # ← 单一 state 实例\n```\n\n`__call__` (L238-272):\n```python\ndef __call__(self, input_ids, scores):\n    batch_size = input_ids.shape[0]\n    for batch_idx in range(batch_size):\n        generated_text = self.tokenizer.decode(input_ids[batch_idx, self.prompt_length:].tolist(), ...)\n        position = self._detect_position(generated_text)  # ← 修改 self.state\n        if position == FieldPosition.RELATION_HEAD_TYPE:\n            scores[batch_idx] = self._mask_head_type(scores[batch_idx])  # ← 读 self.state\n```\n\n**问题**：`_detect_position()` 调用 `PositionDetector.detect(generated_text, self.state)` 会**修改** `self.state`（entity_registry、current_relation_type、current_head_type 等）。Beam search 下 batch_size = num_beams，循环中每个 beam 的 parsing 都会**覆盖**同一个 state。\n\n具体影响：\n- Beam 0 注册 entity \"BERT\" → type \"Method\"，写入 `self.state.entity_registry`\n- Beam 1 解析时可能尚未生成 \"BERT\"，但 registry 已污染\n- `_mask_head_type()` / `_mask_tail_type()` 读取 `self.state.current_relation_type` 时得到的是最后一个 beam 的值\n\n---\n\n## 4. Sampling 的可行性\n\nSampling（`do_sample=True, temperature=T, top_p=P`）在 **batch_size=1** 下执行，`input_ids.shape[0]` 始终为 1，不存在 state 冲突。\n\n**所需改动（纯参数传递，不涉及 processor 逻辑）：**\n\n```python\n# argparse 加 3 个参数\nparser.add_argument(\"--do_sample\", action=\"store_true\")\nparser.add_argument(\"--temperature\", type=float, default=1.0)\nparser.add_argument(\"--top_p\", type=float, default=1.0)\n\n# generate() 调用改为\ngen_kwargs = dict(**inputs, max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,\n                  do_sample=args.do_sample,\n                  temperature=args.temperature,\n                  top_p=args.top_p)\n```\n\n改动量：~10 行。\n\n---\n\n## 5. Beam Search 所需改动\n\n### 核心改动：per-beam state\n\n`TypeConstraintProcessor` 需要维护 `List[GenerationState]` 而非单一 `GenerationState`：\n\n```python\nclass TypeConstraintProcessor(LogitsProcessor):\n    def __init__(self, spec, tokenizer, prompt_length=0, num_beams=1):\n        self.states = [GenerationState() for _ in range(num_beams)]\n\n    def __call__(self, input_ids, scores):\n        if len(self.states) < input_ids.shape[0]:\n            # 延迟初始化到实际 beam 数\n            self.states = [GenerationState() for _ in range(input_ids.shape[0])]\n\n        for batch_idx in range(input_ids.shape[0]):\n            generated_text = self.tokenizer.decode(...)\n            position = self.detector.detect(generated_text, self.states[batch_idx])\n            # masking 函数也需接受 state 参数\n            if position == FieldPosition.RELATION_HEAD_TYPE:\n                scores[batch_idx] = self._mask_head_type(scores[batch_idx], self.states[batch_idx])\n```\n\n### 改动清单\n\n| 文件 | 改动 |\n|------|------|\n| `compiler/type_mask_processor.py` | `self.state` → `self.states: List[GenerationState]`；`_mask_head_type`/`_mask_tail_type` 加 state 参数 |\n| `compiler/type_mask_processor_cf_fallback.py` | 同上（相同结构） |\n| `run_constrained_extraction.py` | 加 `--num_beams` 参数，传入 generate() |\n\n### 改动量估算\n\n- `type_mask_processor.py`: ~30 行改动（state 初始化 + 函数签名 + 索引）\n- `type_mask_processor_cf_fallback.py`: ~30 行（相同改动）\n- `run_constrained_extraction.py`: ~15 行（argparse + gen_kwargs）\n- **总计：~75 行**\n\n### 额外注意\n\n- HF `generate()` 在 beam search 中可能做 beam reordering（选出 top-k beams 后重排序）。此时 `input_ids` 的顺序会变化，`states` 列表需要对应重排。不过由于 `PositionDetector.detect()` 每步都从完整 generated_text 重新解析（无增量 state），所以 reordering 不会导致 state 不一致——每步都是从文本重建 state。**这意味着 per-beam state 实际上不需要跨步维护**，因为 detect() 每次 full re-parse。\n- 但 `entity_registry` 是累积的——每步 regex finditer 全文，所以即使 beam reorder 也能正确重建。\n\n**结论：改动后 beam search 直接可行，无需处理 beam reorder。**\n\n---\n\n## 6. 总结\n\n| 场景 | 可行性 | 改动量 | 风险 |\n|------|--------|--------|------|\n| Sampling (temp/top_p) | ✅ 立即可用 | ~10 行 | 无 |\n| Beam search (num_beams) | ⚠️ 需改代码 | ~75 行 | 低（detect() 每步全文 re-parse，无增量 state 依赖） |\n\n**推荐回复 reviewer**：sampling 已支持（或极易添加），beam search 需约 75 行改动使 TypeConstraintProcessor 支持 per-beam state，架构上无阻塞。\n",

        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_6/projects/schema_semantic_constrained_ie/artifacts/entity_type_collision_diagnosis.md",
        "content": "## Entity Type Tokenization 分析\n\n### 背景\nWikiEvents 27 种 entity types（全大写 3-5 字母缩写）：\nABS, AML, BAL, BOD, COM, CRM, EVENT, FAC, GPE, INF, LAW, LOC, MHI, MON, NAT, ORG, PER, PLA, PTH, RES, SEN, SID, SIDE, TTL, VAL, VEH, WEA\n\n---\n\n### Mistral tokenizer (SentencePiece, LlamaTokenizerFast, vocab=32768)\n\n| Entity Type | Bare 1st (token) | Gen-context 1st (token) | Match? |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| ABS | 1098 ('A') | 2960 ('AB') | MISMATCH |\n| AML | 1098 ('A') | 3622 ('AM') | MISMATCH |\n| BAL | 1133 ('B') | **29528 ('B')** | MISMATCH |\n| BOD | 1133 ('B') | **29528 ('B')** | MISMATCH |\n| COM | 16692 ('COM') | 19219 ('COM') | MISMATCH |\n| CRM | 12775 ('CR') | 6496 ('CR') | MISMATCH |\n| EVENT | 19461 ('EVENT') | 11953 ('EVENT') | MISMATCH |\n| FAC | 1169 ('F') | 29533 ('F') | MISMATCH |\n| GPE | 1188 ('G') | 29545 ('G') | MISMATCH |\n| INF | 3461 ('IN') | 1543 ('IN') | MISMATCH |\n| LAW | 16552 ('LA') | 9191 ('LA') | MISMATCH |\n| LOC | 7575 ('LO') | 10436 ('LOC') | MISMATCH |\n| MHI | 1119 ('M') | 29523 ('M') | MISMATCH |\n| MON | 26027 ('MON') | 18117 ('MON') | MISMATCH |\n| NAT | 25367 ('NAT') | 29527 ('N') | MISMATCH |\n| ORG | 4762 ('OR') | 1785 ('OR') | MISMATCH |\n| PER | 22783 ('PER') | 7280 ('PER') | MISMATCH |\n| PLA | 1135 ('P') | 4666 ('PL') | MISMATCH |\n| PTH | 1135 ('P') | 29521 ('P') | MISMATCH |\n| RES | 21076 ('RES') | 9972 ('RES') | MISMATCH |\n| SEN | 1086 ('S') | **29503 ('S')** | MISMATCH |\n| SID | 1086 ('S') | **29503 ('S')** | MISMATCH |\n| SIDE | 1086 ('S') | **29503 ('S')** | MISMATCH |\n| TTL | 1088 ('T') | 29506 ('T') | MISMATCH |\n| VAL | 19548 ('VAL') | 4436 ('VAL') | MISMATCH |\n| VEH | 1318 ('V') | 4496 ('VE') | MISMATCH |\n| WEA | 1162 ('W') | 7399 ('WE') | MISMATCH |\n\n**ALL 27 entity types** have bare ≠ generation-context token IDs（SentencePiece 上下文依赖）。\n\n### First-token collision 矩阵 (Mistral, generation context)\n\n| First Token | Token ID | Colliding Entity Types |\n|---|---|---|\n| 'S' | 29503 | **SEN, SID, SIDE** |\n| 'B' | 29528 | **BAL, BOD** |\n\n2 个碰撞组，5/27 types 受影响。\n\n### Bare encoding collision groups (compile_type_constraints 会崩溃)\n\n| First Token | Token ID | Colliding Entity Types |\n|---|---|---|\n| 'A' | 1098 | ABS, AML |\n| 'B' | 1133 | BAL, BOD |\n| 'P' | 1135 | PLA, PTH |\n| 'S' | 1086 | SEN, SID, SIDE |\n\n4 个碰撞组，9/27 types。**`compile_type_constraints()` 在 Mistral 上抛 ValueError**。\n`run_constrained_extraction.py` 通过 `ensure_first_token_map()` 绕过了碰撞检查。\n\n### Prefix map 覆盖检查\n\n- `_build_entity_type_prefix_map()` 做全词表扫描 → **所有 generation-context first tokens 都在 prefix map 中**\n- D044 修复的方法（vocab scan）对 entity type 确实有效覆盖\n- 问题不是 token 缺失，而是碰撞组内 token 共享\n\n### 碰撞影响分析\n\n**103/245 (42%)** 的 (event_type, role) 约束与碰撞组有 partial overlap：\n- 51/56 event types 受影响\n- 主要模式：SID 被允许但 SEN/SIDE 不被允许的 agent role（Attacker, Damager, Victor, Communicator, Destroyer 等等）\n- 次要模式：BAL 被允许但 BOD 不被允许的 artifact role（Artifact, Instrument 等）\n\n当约束说 \"只允许 SID\" 时：\n1. Mask 看到 token 29503 ('S') 可达 {SEN, SID, SIDE}，SID ∈ allowed → 不阻塞\n2. Model 生成 'S' 后，XGrammar 允许 'EN'/'ID'/'IDE' 三种延续\n3. Semantic mask 只在 entity type 首 token 生效，对后续 token 无约束\n4. → SEN 或 SIDE 可能生成 → AC violation\n\n---\n\n### Qwen tokenizer (BPE, Qwen2TokenizerFast, vocab=151643)\n\n- **0 bare vs generation-context mismatches**\n- **0 collision groups**\n- BPE tokenizer 上下文无关 → entity type masking 完全有效\n\n---\n\n### Llama tokenizer (BPE, PreTrainedTokenizerFast, vocab=128256)\n\n- **0 bare vs generation-context mismatches**\n- **0 collision groups**\n- `compile_type_constraints()` 不崩溃\n- Entity type masking 正常工作\n\n---\n\n### 根因确认\n\nMistral AC=16.6% 根因是 **(A) first-token collision**：\n\n- **不是 (B)**: in-context token 不在 prefix map → 已被 D044 全词表扫描方法解决\n- **是 (A)**: SEN/SID/SIDE 和 BAL/BOD 碰撞组共享 generation-context 首 token → mask 无法区分组内 types → 42% 约束槽位失效\n- 伴随问题: `compile_type_constraints()` 对 Mistral 崩溃（bare encoding 4 组碰撞），`ensure_first_token_map()` 绕过检查但不解决问题\n\nQwen AC=2.1%：BPE 无碰撞，残余 AC 来自其他因素（grammar-first fallback、position detection 边界情况）\n\nLlama AC=3.4%：BPE 无碰撞，残余 AC 同上\n\n---\n\n### 修复建议\n\n**方案 1（推荐）: 碰撞组专用 continuation mask**\n在 `_apply_type_mask` 检测到碰撞组首 token 未被阻塞时，注册一个 continuation handler：下一步在碰撞组成员的第二 token 之间做 masking。例如 SID 允许时，下一步阻塞 'EN' (→SEN) 和 'IDE' (→SIDE) 的 token，只允许 'ID' (→SID)。\n\n**方案 2: 多 token trie masking**\n将 `_apply_type_mask` 从单步首 token 扩展为多步 trie 约束。每步检查已生成前缀 + 当前 token 是否仍可达任何 allowed type。需要跨步骤维护 partial match state。\n\n**方案 3（最简单）: 重命名碰撞 entity types**\n修改 ontology 中碰撞 types 的名称使首 token 唯一化：\n- SEN → SENT（首 token 'S' → 'SE'... 仍碰撞 SID）\n- 或 SEN → SENTENCE / SID → SIDEG / SIDE → SIDEA\n- BAL → BALL / BOD → BODY\n这需要同时修改 ontology JSON、prompt 模板和 violation checker。\n\n**方案 4: 对 Mistral 禁用 semantic mask 对碰撞组的约束**\n不修复碰撞，但准确报告 \"semantic mask 覆盖率 = (245-103)/245 = 58%\"，论文中标注此限制。\n",
        "numLines": 133,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 133
      }
    },

  },

      "- Explore: Fast read-only search agent for locating code. Use it to find files by pattern (eg. \"src/components/**/*.tsx\"), grep for symbols or keywords (eg. \"API endpoints\"), or answer \"where is X defined / which files reference Y.\" Do NOT use it for code review, design-doc auditing, cross-file consistency checks, or open-ended analysis — it reads excerpts rather than whole files and will miss content past its read window.       "- general-purpose: General-purpose agent for researching complex questions, searching for code, and executing multi-step tasks.
